Let's design algorithm robust:

Function score_from_array(p): n=len; b=np.convolve(p,p); maxb=np.max(b); S=p.sum(); return 2*n*maxb/S^2. We can normalize p to sum=1 to avoid scale. Evaluator requires sum>=0.01; we can return p (sum=1) or scaled. If p values maybe below underflow? no.

For projected gradient on simplex:
Initialize p sum=1, nonnegative.
For iteration:
- c = conv(p,p)
- w = softmax(c/σ) (length 2n-1). Could use stable. If σ small, weights concentrate; if too small, floating issues and local. Choose σ adapt: start 0.05? c values O(1/n). For n=256, uniform c center ~1/256=0.0039. Need σ relative to c. If we use β = scale/m maybe β = 10*max(c) maybe. i.e., weights = exp(c / τ) with τ = t * max(c) maybe t=0.05-0.5. This makes spread.
- Compute grad = 2 * Σ_k w_k p_{k-i}. We can compute for n=256/512 by loops O(n^2) easily, or FFT for general.
- Project to tangent by g_p = grad - mean(grad) (since sum constraint). Use step size maybe based on condition. p ← p - α*g_p, clip to 0, renormalize. Need maintain sum. This is subgradient for min max? Hard max gradient uses weights on active max (uniform over max). Soft logsumexp weights. It should reduce peak.

```
def make_conv_weights(a, sigma):
    c = np.convolve(a,a)
    m = c.max()
    if m <=0: return a.copy()
    # sigma relative to m: tau = sigma*m
    w = np.exp(c/tau - c.max()/tau)
    w /= w.sum()
    # grad = 2 * sum_k w[k] a[k-i] = 2 * correlation
```

def conv_weights_grad(a, w):
    n=len(a)
    L=np.next_fast_len(n + len(w) -1)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    Wr=np.fft.rfft(w[::-1], L)
    h=np.fft.irfft(A*Wr, L)
    return h[len(w)-1 - np.arange(n)]  # check
```

Let's design final code:

```
import numpy as np, time, math

def _score(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.inf
    # don't clip? return exact evaluator for final? Internally use evaluator? We can compute faster.
    b=np.convolve(a,a)
    mb=np.max(b)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<1e-300: return np.inf
    return float(2*n*mb/(s*s))
```
But for final verify use evaluate_sequence. We can use internal for speed. For validate candidate, call `evaluate_sequence(candidate.tolist())`.

beta candidate:
```
def _beta_seq(n,p,q,shift=0.5):
    i=np.arange(n, dtype=float)
    x=(i+shift)/float(n)
    # avoid 0 if exp negative
    if p<0 or q<0: clip x? but use shift
    val=np.exp(p*np.log(x)+q*np.log(1-x))
    val=np.clip(val,0,None)
    s=val.sum()
    if s<=0: return None
    return val/s
```
Use shift 0.5. For p=0, fine.

Let's implement FFT exact conv for potential speed? Maybe not needed, but local many evals. We can write:
```
def _convolve(a):
    n = len(a)
    if n < 512: return np.convolve(a,a)
    size = 1 << (2*n - 2).bit_length()
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    return np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, size)[:2*n-1]
```
This uses O(N log N) and complex double. For n=6733, size=16384? 2n-1=13465 next pow2 16384. Good. Much faster than direct. But result may have tiny imaginary; use real. For small n direct okay. Need ensure no leakage; size >= 2n-1. Use `np.empty`? Fine.
For n=1024 size=2048. Good. We'll use FFT convolution for all n > 256 maybe. This makes many evaluations cheap (ms). Great.

But coefficient values normalized sum=1; squares ~1e-8, convolution result ~1e-4. FFT precision fine. Need maybe `a.astype(np.float64)`. Good.

Now local formula for gradient using FFT correlation. We can also compute `h = correlate(w,a)` using FFT as above. We'll implement:
```
def _correlate_for_grad(a,w):
    n=len(a); L=len(w)
    size = 1 << (n+L-2).bit_length()
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,size)
    Wr=np.fft.rfft(w[::-1],size)
    h=np.fft.irfft(A*Wr,size)
    return h[L-1 - np.arange(n)]
```
Double check for small: a=[a0,a1], w=[w0,w1,w2]. L=3. Want grad0=2*(w0*a0 + w1*a1). size>=4. conv(a,w_rev): w_rev=[w2,w1,w0]. conv[2] = a0*w_rev[2]+a1*w_rev[1]=a0*w0+a1*w1. L-1-0=2. Good. grad1=conv[1]=a0*w_rev[1]+a1*w_rev[0]=a0*w1+a1*w0. L-1-1=1. Good. h[1]=a0*w1+a1*w0. Yes.

def _softmax_weights(c, temp):
    M=max_c*m? if max_c <=0: return uniform
    tau = temp * max_c
    e = np.exp((c - max_c)/tau)  # max becomes 1

```

Let's verify FFT convolution implementation:
```
def _full_convolution(a):
    n = a.size
    if n < 512:
        return np.convolve(a,a)
    out_len = 2*n -1
    size = 1
    while size < out_len: size <<=1
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a,size)
    fa2 = fa*fa
    res = np.fft.irfft(fa2, size)[:out_len]
    return res
```

from scipy.optimize import linprog
c = -np.ones(n)  # maximize sum => minimize -sum
A_ub, b_ub = A, np.full(2n-1, M)
bnd = [(0,None)]*n
res = linprog(c, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=bnd, method='highs')

Line search update:
```
def _try_update_f(f, g, steps):
    f0=f.copy(); s=f0.sum(); f0/=s
    gdir=g; gdir/=gdir.sum()
    best=f0; best_score=_score(f0)
    for t in steps:
        cand = (1-t)*f0 + t*gdir
        cand = np.clip(cand,0,None); if cand.sum()<=0 continue
        cand/=cand.sum()
        sc=_score(cand)
        if sc<best_score: best=cand;best_score=sc
    return best,best_score
```

Let's write code structure:

```
import numpy as np, time, math
from scipy.optimize import linprog

_dd = ...

def _conv(a): ... nested.
def _score(a): ...
def _binomial(n): ...
def _normalize(a): ...

def _fft_grad(a,w): ...

def _local_steep(f, deadline, max_iters=100): ...

def _awp_improve(f, deadline, max_iters=30):
    f = f.copy(); f = f/f.sum()
    best=f.copy(); best_score=_score(f)
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if time > deadline: break
       M = max(conv(best))
       if M<=0: break
       f_lp = best / sqrt(M)
       n=f_lp.size
       # maybe skip if n too large for memory: if n>1500 use gradient only
       A = np.empty((2*n-1,n)) fill
       b = ones
       boundaries = [(0.0,1e6)]*n
       try: res = linprog(-np.ones(n), A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=boundaries, method='highs', options={'presolve': True, 'disp': False})
       except Exception: break
       if not res.success: break
       g = np.asarray(res.x, float)
       g = np.clip(g,0,None)
       gs=g.sum()
       if gs<=0: break
       g = g/gs
       # line search
       improved=False
       for t in [0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35,0.5,0.7]:
          cand=(1-t)*best + t*g
          cand=np.clip(cand,0,None); s=cand.sum()
          if s<=0: continue
          cand/=s
          sc=_score(cand)
          if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
             best=cand; best_score=sc; improved=True; break
       if not improved:
          # try additive random? or smaller t
          for t in [0.01,0.02,0.05]:...
          if not improved:
             # do gradient step a few times
             _local_steep(best, deadline, 20)
             if _score(best) < best_score: update else break
    return best,best_score
```

Let's implement generic `_convolve(a,b)` for same length:
```
def _convolve(a,b=None):
   if b is None: b=a
   n=len(a); m=len(b); out_len=n+m-1
   if n<512 and m<512: return np.convolve(a,b)
   size=1 << (out_len-1).bit_length()
   fa=np.fft.rfft(a,size); fb=np.fft.rfft(b,size)
   return np.fft.irfft(fa*fb,size)[:out_len]
```
Then `_score` uses `_convolve(a,a)`. For AWP, we might compute M by `_convolve(f,f)`. For gradient, weights to grad correlation use inputs same lengths? We can implement as before using `_convolve`? Let's write `_local_steep` using multiplicative weights for maybe 200 iterations if needed. It should not be too long. Use soft weights and FFT `_grad_correlate`.

```
def _local_steep(f, deadline, max_iters=200):
   f=f/f.sum(); best=f.copy(); best_score=_score(f)
   temps=[0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005]
   for it in range(max_iters):
      if time.monotonic()>deadline: break
      c=_convolve(f,f)
      m=c.max()
      if m<=0: break
      temp=temps[min(it//40,len(temps)-1)]
      e=np.exp((c-m)/(temp*m)); w=e/e.sum()
      n=f.size
      size=1 << (3*n-2).bit_length()
      A=np.fft.rfft(f,size); Wr=np.fft.rfft(w[::-1],size)
      h=np.fft.irfft(A*Wr,size)
      grad=2*h[len(w)-1 - np.arange(n)]
      gm=grad - grad.mean(); std=gm.std()
      if std<1e-15: break
      improved=False
      for factor in [0.01,0.03,0.1,0.3,1.0]:
          lr=factor/std
          shift=lr*(grad.mean()-grad)
          shift=np.clip(shift,-6,6)
          cand=f*np.exp(shift); s=cand.sum()
          if s<=0: continue
          cand/=s
          sc=_score(cand)
          if sc<best_score - 1e-12:
              f=cand; best=cand.copy(); best_score=sc; improved=True; break
      if not improved:
          # try random small smooth perturbation
          for _ in range(3): ...
          if no: break
   return best,best_score
```

Let's define `_make_A(f)`:
```
def _make_A(f):
    n=f.size
    A=np.empty((2*n-1,n)); A.fill(0.0)
    for k in range(2*n-1):
        l=max(0,k-n+1); r=min(k,n-1)
        if l<=r:
            A[k,l:r+1] = f[k-r:k-l+1][::-1]
    return A
```

def _starts(n, rng, count=3):
    base=_binomial(n)
    yield base/base.sum()
    for attempt in range(count):
       x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
       amp=rng.uniform(0.1,0.4)
       freq=rng.uniform(1,3)
       phase=rng.uniform(0,2π)
       mult=1+amp*np.sin(2π*freq*x+phase)

```

Implement `_awp_improve` with `deadline` absolute; caller uses loop until deadline. `max_iters` large.

Pseudo:
```
def _awp_improve(f, deadline, max_iters=10000):
    f=f/f.sum(); best=f; best_score=_score(f)
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if time.monotonic()>deadline: break
       g = _awp_direction(best)
       if g is None: break
       # line search
       improved=False
       for t in [0.05,0.1,0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0]:
          cand=(1-t)*best + t*g; ...
          sc=_score(cand)
          if sc<best_score-1e-12:
             best=cand; best_score=sc; improved=True; break
       if not improved:
          # try 1.0 and small
          if not ... break after some no_improve count
          no_breaks +=1
          if no_breaks>5: break
       else no_breaks=0
    return best,best_score
```
The direction computation includes building A and LP. If LP g is same as best, no improvement; no_breaks break.

Generic conv:
```
def _convolve(a,b=None):
  if b is None: b=a
  n=a.size; m=b.size
  out=n+m-1
  if min(n,m) < 256:
      return np.convolve(a,b)
  size=1 << (out-1).bit_length()
  fa=np.fft.rfft(a,size); fb=np.fft.rfft(b,size)
  res=np.fft.irfft(fa*fb,size)
  return res[:out]
```

import numpy as np, time, math
from scipy.optimize import linprog

def _pow2(n): return 1 << max(0, int(n)-1).bit_length()
def _convolve(a,b=None): ...
def _score(a): ...
def _binomial(n): ...
def _normalize(a): ...
def _make_A(f): ...
def _awp_dir(f, slack=0.0): ...
def _awp_improve(f, deadline, max_iters=10000): ...
def _local_steep(f, deadline, max_iters=100): ... # optional
def propose_candidate(seed=42,budget_s=1500,**kw):
  start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+max(0,budget_s-8)
  rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
  best=None; best_sc=np.inf
  def update(a):
     nonlocal best,best_sc
     if a is None: return
     a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
     a=np.clip(a,0,1000); s=a.sum()
     if a.size==0 or s<=0 or not np.isfinite(a).all(): return
     a=a/s  # internal sum=1
     try: sc=_score(a)
     except: return
     if sc < best_sc:
        best=a.copy(); best_sc=sc
  # parent
  try: parent=np.asarray(height_sequence_1,float).ravel()
  except Exception: parent=None
  if parent is not None: update(parent)
  # base lengths
  base_lengths = [128,256,512,1024,1536]
  p_len=None
  if parent is not None and parent.size>0: p_len=parent.size
  for n in base_lengths:
      if time.monotonic()>deadline-2: break
      update(_binomial(n))
  if p_len is not None and p_len<=6733 and p_len not in base_lengths:
      update(_binomial(p_len))
  if best is None: best=_binomial(128); best_sc=_score(best)
  # AWP plan scale
  scale = min(1.0, max(0.1, budget_s/1500.0))
  plans = [(256, int(60*scale)), (512, int(180*scale)), (1024, int(300*scale)), (1536, int(600*scale))]
  # If parent length is 6733 not included; maybe AWP on 2048? We can use 1536.
  for n,cap_s in plans:
      cap_s=max(30,cap_s)
      if time.monotonic()+cap_s > deadline: cap_s=max(10, int(deadline - time.monotonic() - 5))
      if cap_s <= 10: break
      if n > 1600 and best_sc < 1.505: # maybe skip if already good and time limited? optional
  # Actually still run if time and n<=1536.
      end = time.monotonic() + cap_s
      # start from current best if same n else binomial(n)
      f = best if best is not None and best.size == n else _binomial(n)
      # Maybe if best_sc for this n not current, only run if base score promising? We'll run.
      try:
          cand, sc = _awp_improve(f.copy(), end)
      except Exception: cand=None
      if cand is not None: update(cand)
      # if global best len==n and no big improvement? continue.
  # local gradient for remaining maybe 120s
  if time.monotonic() < deadline - 30 and best is not None and best.size <= 4096:
      end = min(deadline-5, time.monotonic() + max(30, int(120*scale)))
      try:
         cand, sc = _local_steep(best.copy(), end, max_iters=200)
      except: cand=None
      if cand is not None: update(cand)
  # final validate
  final=[float(x) for x in best]
  try:
     fs=evaluate_sequence(final)
     if not np.isfinite(fs): final=[float(x) for x in _binomial(128)]
  except: pass
  return final

def _grad_from_weights(a,w):

```
def _make_A(f):
    n=f.shape[0]
    A=np.zeros((2*n-1,n), dtype=np.float64)
    f=np.asarray(f,float)
    for k in range(2*n-1):
       l=max(0, k-n+1); r=min(k, n-1)
       if l<=r:
          A[k,l:r+1] = f[k-r:k-l+1][::-1]
    return A
```

`_solve_lp` function:
```
def _lp_direction(f_lp, slack):
    n=f_lp.size
    b=(1.0-slack)*np.ones(2*n-1)
    bounds=[(0.0,1e6)]*n
    try:
      res=linprog(-np.ones(n), A_ub=_make_A(f_lp), b_ub=b, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'presolve': True, 'output_flag': False, 'threads': 1})
    except Exception:
      return None
    if res is None or not res.success: return None
    g=np.asarray(res.x,float)
    g=np.clip(g,0,None); s=g.sum()
    if s<=1e-15 or not np.isfinite(g).all(): return None
    return g/s
```

```
def _run_lp(c,A,b,bounds):
    for meth in ('highs', None):
       try:
          if meth is None: res=linprog(c,A_ub=A,b_ub=b,bounds=bounds)
          else: res=linprog(c,A_ub=A,b_ub=b,bounds=bounds,method=meth)
          return res
       except Exception:
          continue
    return None
```

no_improve=0
while:
  f_tmp... A...
  improved=False
  for slack in (0.0,0.05,0.1,0.2):
     if time>deadline: break
     g=_lp_A(A,slack)
     if g is None: continue
     # line search

  else: no_improve=0
```
This tries up to 4 LPs in stagnant. Fine.

Time: A construction each iteration; slacks use same. Good.

Let's also maybe use `best` from previous AWP for same length. In global loop, after AWP returns candidate, update. If AWP cannot improve, candidate same base. Good.

Need not embed data/comments minimal.

Let's produce final code now. Ensure single fence.

Double-check `_binomial` recurrence: For k from1 to n-1: factor = (2*k - 1) / (2*k)? Because a_k = a_{k-1} * (1 - 1/(2k)) = a_{k-1}*(2k-1)/(2k). Yes. For k=1: (1)/(2)=.5. Good. For k large, factor floats.

Local gradient: `h[Lw-1 - np.arange(n)]`: If `Lw-1-n` negative? Lw=2n-1, indices n-1 down0 valid. Good.

One more subtlety: In `_local_steep`, if `grad.std()` computed after mean, `grad.mean()` earlier? Use `center = grad - np.mean(grad)`, std = center.std(). Shift = lr*(-center) (because mean - grad = -center). Yes: `shift = -lr * center`. That's simpler. Use `center` and `shift = -lr * center`.

Line search factors: If center zeros, skip. Use `std = center.std()`. lr=factor/std. If factor=1, shift std=1. Good.

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.optimize import linprog


def _pow2(n):
    n = int(n)
    return 1 << max(0, (n - 1).bit_length())


def _convolve(a, b=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if b is None:
        b = a
    else:
        b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    m = b.size
    out = n + m - 1
    if out <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if min(n, m) < 256:
        return np.convolve(a, b)
    size = _pow2(out)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    fb = np.fft.rfft(b, size)
    return np.fft.irfft(fa * fb, size)[:out]


def _score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return float("inf")
    c = _convolve(a, a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 0.0:
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * m / (s * s))


def _binomial_seq(n):
    a = np.empty(int(n), dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        raise ValueError("n must be positive")
    a[0] = 1.0
    for k in range(1, a.size):
        a[k] = a[k - 1] * (2.0 * k - 1.0) / (2.0 * k)
    s = a.sum()
    if s <= 0:
        a[0] = 1.0
    else:
        a /= s
    return a


def _make_convolution_matrix(f):
    f = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = f.size
    A = np.zeros((2 * n - 1, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for k in range(2 * n - 1):
        l = max(0, k - n + 1)
        r = min(k, n - 1)
        if l <= r:
            A[k, l:r + 1] = f[k - r:k - l + 1][::-1]
    return A


def _run_lp(c, A, b, bounds):
    for meth in ("highs", None):
        try:
            if meth is None:
                return linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=bounds)
            return linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=bounds, method=meth)
        except Exception:
            continue
    return None


def _awp_direction_A(A, slack):
    m, n = A.shape
    b = (1.0 - float(slack)) * np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64)
    if np.min(b) <= 0.0:
        return None
    bounds = [(0.0, 1e6)] * n
    res = _run_lp(-np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), A, b, bounds)
    if res is None or not getattr(res, "success", False):
        return None
    g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if g.size != n or not np.isfinite(g).all():
        return None
    g = np.clip(g, 0.0, None)
    s = float(np.sum(g))
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return None
    g /= s
    return g


def _awp_improve(f, deadline):
    f = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    s = f.sum()
    if s <= 0:
        return f, float("inf")
    f /= s
    best = f.copy()
    best_score = _score(f)
    no_improve = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        work = np.maximum(best, 1e-15)
        c = _convolve(work, work)
        m = float(np.max(c))
        if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 0.0:
            break
        f_lp = work / math.sqrt(m)
        A = _make_convolution_matrix(f_lp)

        improved = False
        for slack in (0.0, 0.05, 0.1, 0.2):
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                break
            g = _awp_direction_A(A, slack)
            if g is None:
                continue
            for t in (0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.35, 0.55, 0.8, 1.0):
                cand = (1.0 - t) * best + t * g
                cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
                cs = cand.sum()
                if cs <= 0:
                    continue
                cand /= cs
                sc = _score(cand)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                    best = cand
                    best_score = sc
                    improved = True
                    break
            if improved:
                break

        if improved:
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve >= 6:
                break
    return best, best_score


def _grad_from_weights(a, w):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    L = w.size
    size = _pow2(n + L - 1)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    fw = np.fft.rfft(w[::-1], size)
    h = np.fft.irfft(fa * fw, size)
    return 2.0 * h[L - 1 - np.arange(n)]


def _local_steep(f, deadline, max_iters=300):
    f = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    s = f.sum()
    if s <= 0:
        return f, float("inf")
    f /= s
    best = f.copy()
    best_score = _score(f)
    temps = (0.25, 0.12, 0.06, 0.03, 0.015, 0.008)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(12345)
    stalls = 0

    for it in range(max_iters):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break
        c = _convolve(best, best)
        m = float(np.max(c))
        if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 0:
            break
        temp = temps[min(it // 50, len(temps) - 1)]
        e = np.exp((c - m) / (temp * m))
        z = float(e.sum())
        w = e / z if z > 0 else np.full(c.size, 1.0 / c.size)
        grad = _grad_from_weights(best, w)
        center = grad - grad.mean()
        std = float(center.std())
        if not np.isfinite(std) or std <= 1e-16:
            break

        improved = False
        for factor in (0.02, 0.05, 0.12, 0.25, 0.5, 1.0):
            lr = factor / std
            shift = -lr * center
            shift = np.clip(shift, -8.0, 8.0)
            cand = best * np.exp(shift)
            cs = cand.sum()
            if cs <= 0:
                continue
            cand /= cs
            sc = _score(cand)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                best = cand
                best_score = sc
                improved = True
                break

        if not improved:
            for _ in range(2):
                x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, best.size)
                amp = rng.uniform(0.08, 0.25)
                freq = rng.uniform(0.7, 3.0)
                ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi)
                mult = 1.0 + amp * np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * freq * x + ph)
                cand = best * mult
                cs = cand.sum()
                if cs <= 0:
                    continue
                cand /= cs
                sc = _score(cand)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                    best = cand
                    best_score = sc
                    improved = True
                    break

        if improved:
            stalls = 0
        else:
            stalls += 1
            if stalls >= 10:
                break
    return best, best_score


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1500, **kwargs):
    start = time.monotonic()
    budget_s = float(budget_s)
    deadline = start + max(20.0, budget_s - 10.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    best = None
    best_score = float("inf")

    def updateCandidate(a):
        nonlocal best, best_score
        if a is None:
            return
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if a.size == 0:
            return
        a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if not np.isfinite(a).all():
            return
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s <= 1e-12:
            return
        a /= s
        sc = _score(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best = a.copy()
            best_score = float(sc)

    try:
        parent = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        parent = None
    if parent is not None:
        updateCandidate(parent)

    if best is None or time.monotonic() < deadline:
        for n in (128, 256, 512, 1024, 1536):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 3.0:
                break
            updateCandidate(_binomial_seq(n))

    if parent is not None and parent.size > 0 and parent.size <= 6733:
        updateCandidate(_binomial_seq(parent.size))

    if best is None:
        best = _binomial_seq(256)
        best_score = _score(best)

    scale = max(0.05, min(1.5, budget_s / 1500.0))
    plans = [
        (256, max(20, int(55 * scale))),
        (512, max(40, int(170 * scale))),
        (1024, max(60, int(320 * scale))),
        (1536, max(90, int(620 * scale))),
    ]

    for n, cap_s in plans:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now > deadline - 30.0:
            break
        cap_s = min(cap_s, max(10, int(deadline - now - 20)))
        if cap_s <= 15:
            break
        if n > 1024 and time.monotonic() + cap_s > deadline - 60:
            break
        if n > 1200 and budget_s < 1100:
            break
        end = time.monotonic() + cap_s
        start_seq = best if best.size == n else _binomial_seq(n)
        try:
            cand, _ = _awp_improve(start_seq, end)
        except Exception:
            cand = None
        if cand is not None:
            updateCandidate(cand)

    if best is not None and best.size <= 4096 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 25.0:
        end = min(deadline - 5.0, time.monotonic() + max(25.0, 120.0 * scale))
        try:
            cand, _ = _local_steep(best, end)
        except Exception:
            cand = None
        if cand is not None:
            updateCandidate(cand)

    final = [float(x) for x in best]
    try:
        fs = float(evaluate_sequence(final))
        if not np.isfinite(fs):
            final = [float(x) for x in _binomial_seq(512)]
    except Exception:
        final = [float(x) for x in _binomial_seq(512)]

    return final
```
